# Code review: skill trees as two pages, menus in the era's look, crosshair, inventory key — Glyphbound (UE 5.9, C++)

You are reviewing, read-only, a small change to the game's canvas HUD (`AHUD::DrawHUD`, no UMG). Run
`git diff HEAD -- Glyphbound/Source` and read the surrounding code in `UI/GBHUD.h`, `UI/GBHUD.cpp`,
`UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp`, `Player/GBInputSet.cpp`, `Player/GBPlayerController.cpp` (how the gameplay and menu mapping
contexts swap, and how the Inventory action toggles the menu) and `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (only the changed smoke
steps). Ignore Devlog files.

What changed (owner's requests):
1. **Skill trees as two pages.** The spell tree and the weapon tree used to be drawn side by side. Now one tree is
   shown at full width, the one of the selected node (`GetShownSkillTree`), with two tabs above it (clickable:
   new `EMenuHitKind::SkillTreeTab`). On the Skill Trees screen, the tab keys (Q / E, L1 / R1 and new L2) toggle
   the tree (`SelectSkillTree`, which remembers the last node of each tree in `FMenuState::TreeCursor`). Arrow
   navigation (`NavigateSkills`) stays inside the shown tree; only on the inventory's Skills page (where Q / E turn
   inventory pages) does left / right past the tree's edge switch tree. The screen opens on the tree of the held
   item (gun = weapons). The grid's cell width is capped and the grid centred.
2. **Menus in the era's look.** `GetMenuSkin()` (past = 90s RPG, future = cyberpunk, from `UGBEraSubsystem`) now
   picks the skin of every menu screen and of the toasts drawn over menus; the in-game HUD still follows the held item.
3. **Crosshair:** the red bar over the gun reticle's top tick is gone; the top tick is full length.
4. **Input:** I opens / closes the inventory (gameplay and menu contexts), L2 (`Gamepad_LeftTrigger`) turns menu pages.
5. Smoke: the "12_weapon_tree" screenshot now sets a weapon node; the UI block takes one more screenshot per skin
   after a tab press.

## What to look for (ranked)
1. Real bugs: a cursor left on a node of the hidden tree (so arrows or Enter act on something not shown), a tab
   click or key that does nothing or flips twice (e.g. the MenuTab axis firing on press and release, L2 being an
   analog trigger that fires repeatedly, MenuTab's direction sign mattering now that it toggles), `SelectSkillTree`
   with data that has only one tree or no nodes, the `for (int32 Tree = ShownTree; Tree == ShownTree; ++Tree)`
   loop, mouse hits recorded for the hidden tree, the inventory's page switch losing `TreeCursor`.
2. Input conflicts: is I used anywhere else (gameplay or menu)? Does adding L2 to the menu context collide with
   anything while a menu is open, or leak into gameplay when a menu closes? Can I and Tab both toggle the inventory
   in the same frame?
3. `GetMenuSkin`: the era subsystem missing (main menu, PIE start), the skin flipping mid-frame between DrawMenus and
   the toasts, anything else in the menus that still calls `GetCurrentSkin()` (held item) and would now disagree.
4. Layout: text or tabs overflowing at 1280x720 or 2560x1440, the tab row's height in both skins, a centred grid
   that can run outside its panel.

Do not suggest style rewrites, renames or new features. For each finding give: file:line, what goes wrong, a
concrete scenario, and a fix. If you find nothing real, say so plainly.

+EGBHudSkin AGBHUD::GetMenuSkin() const
+{
+	// Menus both eras share (inventory, pause, skill trees, death) take the look of the era the player is in
+	// (owner, Nov 13): the past is the 90s RPG, the future is cyberpunk. The in-game HUD keeps following the held item.
+	if (const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
+	{
+		return Era->GetCurrentEra() == EGBEra::Future ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
+	}
+	return GetCurrentSkin();
+}

+int32 AGBHUD::GetShownSkillTree() const
+{
+	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
+	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
+	return Current ? Current->Tree : 0;
+}
+
+void AGBHUD::SelectSkillTree(int32 Tree)
+{
+	Tree = FMath::Clamp(Tree, 0, 1);
+	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
+	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
+	if (Current)
+	{
+		if (Current->Tree == Tree)
+		{
+			return;
+		}
+		MenuState.TreeCursor[Current->Tree] = Current->Id;
+	}
+	const FName Remembered = MenuState.TreeCursor[Tree];
+	const FSkillNodeRef* Target = Nodes.FindByPredicate([Remembered, Tree](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Tree == Tree && Node.Id == Remembered; });
+	if (!Target)
+	{
+		Target = Nodes.FindByPredicate([Tree](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Tree == Tree; });
+	}
+	if (Target)
+	{
+		MenuState.SkillCursor = Target->Id;
+	}
+	else if (!Current && Nodes.Num() > 0)
+	{
+		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes[0].Id;
 	}
 }

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"

#include "InputAction.h"
#include "InputMappingContext.h"
#include "InputModifiers.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"

namespace
{
	// Stick dead zones (input feel, not gameplay numbers).
	constexpr float GameplayStickDeadZone = 0.2f;
	constexpr float MenuStickDeadZone = 0.5f;

	/** Where a digital key pushes a 2D axis. */
	enum class EDir2D : uint8 { Right, Left, Up, Down };

	UInputAction* MakeAction(UObject* Outer, const TCHAR* Name, EInputActionValueType Type, bool bTriggerWhenPaused = false)
	{
		UInputAction* Action = NewObject<UInputAction>(Outer, MakeUniqueObjectName(Outer, UInputAction::StaticClass(), FName(Name)));
		Action->ValueType = Type;
		Action->bTriggerWhenPaused = bTriggerWhenPaused;
		return Action;
	}

	UInputModifierNegate* MakeNegate(UObject* Outer)
	{
		return NewObject<UInputModifierNegate>(Outer);
	}

	UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* MakeSwizzleYXZ(UObject* Outer)
	{
		UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* Swizzle = NewObject<UInputModifierSwizzleAxis>(Outer);
		Swizzle->Order = EInputAxisSwizzle::YXZ;
		return Swizzle;
	}

	UInputModifierDeadZone* MakeDeadZone(UObject* Outer, float Lower)
	{
		UInputModifierDeadZone* DeadZone = NewObject<UInputModifierDeadZone>(Outer);
		DeadZone->LowerThreshold = Lower;
		DeadZone->UpperThreshold = 1.f;
		DeadZone->Type = EDeadZoneType::Radial;
		return DeadZone;
	}

	// MapKey returns a reference into the context's array: every helper adds its modifiers before the next MapKey.

	void MapButton(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key)
	{
		Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
	}

	/** Axis1D: +1 or -1. */
	void MapAxis1D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, bool bNegative = false)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (bNegative)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a digital key: x = right, y = up / forward. */
	void MapDir2D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, EDir2D Dir)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Up || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeSwizzleYXZ(Context));
		}
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Left || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a stick, with a radial dead zone. */
	void MapStick(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, float DeadZone)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeDeadZone(Context, DeadZone));
	}

	void MapWASD(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action)
	{
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::W, EDir2D::Up);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::S, EDir2D::Down);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::A, EDir2D::Left);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::D, EDir2D::Right);
	}
}

void UGBInputSet::Build()
{
	if (GameplayContext)
	{
		return; // built once per player
	}

	using EType = EInputActionValueType;

	// ---- Actions ----
	Move = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Move"), EType::Axis2D);
	Look = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Look"), EType::Axis2D);
	LookStick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_LookStick"), EType::Axis2D);
	SwitchWeapon = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SwitchWeapon"), EType::Axis1D);
	CycleQuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_CycleQuickUse"), EType::Axis1D);

	Jump = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Jump"), EType::Boolean);
	Dash = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Dash"), EType::Boolean);
	Primary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Primary"), EType::Boolean);
	Secondary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Secondary"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot1 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot1"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot2 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot2"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot3 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot3"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot4 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot4"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot5 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot5"), EType::Boolean);
	Enchant = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Enchant"), EType::Boolean);
	Reload = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Reload"), EType::Boolean);
	Interact = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Interact"), EType::Boolean);
	QuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QuickUse"), EType::Boolean);
	Block = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Block"), EType::Boolean);
	// These three open / close menus, so they must also fire while the game is paused.
	Inventory = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Inventory"), EType::Boolean, true);
	SkillTrees = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SkillTrees"), EType::Boolean, true);
	Pause = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Pause"), EType::Boolean, true);

	FaceUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceUp"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceLeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceLeft"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceDown"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceRight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceRight"), EType::Boolean);

	QTEUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEUp"), EType::Boolean);
	QTELeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTELeft"), EType::Boolean);
	QTEDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEDown"), EType::Boolean);
	QTERight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTERight"), EType::Boolean);

	MenuNavigate = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuNavigate"), EType::Axis2D, true);
	MenuConfirm = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuConfirm"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuBack = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuBack"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuTab = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuTab"), EType::Axis1D, true);
	MenuClick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuClick"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuReset = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuReset"), EType::Boolean, true);

	// ---- Gameplay context (GDD 9) ----
	GameplayContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Gameplay")));
	UInputMappingContext* G = GameplayContext;

	MapWASD(G, Move);
	MapStick(G, Move, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	G->MapKey(Look, EKeys::Mouse2D);
	MapStick(G, LookStick, EKeys::Gamepad_Right2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::MouseWheelAxis);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::LeftAlt);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up);

	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::LeftShift);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, true);

	MapButton(G, Jump, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(G, Dash, EKeys::LeftControl);

	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::Gamepad_RightTrigger);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot2, EKeys::One);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot3, EKeys::Two);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot4, EKeys::Three);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot5, EKeys::Four);

	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::T);
	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, Reload, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(G, Interact, EKeys::G);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::F);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);
	// L2 is also Secondary (aim with a gun, spell modifier with the staff): both meanings coexist, the character
	// and the spellbook decide (block is refused with a gun in hand).
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Q);
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::I);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(G, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);

	MapButton(G, FaceUp, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);
	MapButton(G, FaceLeft, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Left);
	MapButton(G, FaceDown, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);
	MapButton(G, FaceRight, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapButton(G, QTEUp, EKeys::W);
	MapButton(G, QTELeft, EKeys::A);
	MapButton(G, QTEDown, EKeys::S);
	MapButton(G, QTERight, EKeys::D);

	// ---- Menu context ----
	MenuContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Menu")));
	UInputMappingContext* M = MenuContext;

	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapWASD(M, MenuNavigate);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapStick(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, MenuStickDeadZone);

	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Enter);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);

	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Q, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::E);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);
	// L2 also turns pages (owner: "L1 y L2" between the two skill trees). In the gameplay context it is aim / block,
	// but that context is not read while a menu is open.
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(M, MenuClick, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);

	// Skill reset shortcut (menu context only): R and the gamepad top face button. Neither is mapped
	// to another menu action (R is Reload only in the gameplay context; the top face button is only
	// a raw FaceUp gameplay input), so no existing menu binding is changed.
	MapButton(M, MenuReset, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(M, MenuReset, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);

	// Inside menus: the gamepad Menu / View buttons and K still toggle their screens (Esc and Tab are "back" here).
	MapButton(M, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::I);
	MapButton(M, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
}

#include "Player/GBPlayerController.h"

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Core/GBGameMode.h"
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

#include "EnhancedInputComponent.h"
#include "EnhancedInputSubsystems.h"
#include "InputActionValue.h"
#include "Engine/LocalPlayer.h"

namespace
{
	// The menu context sits above the gameplay one, so during the death screen (both may be active) menu keys win.
	constexpr int32 GameplayContextPriority = 0;
	constexpr int32 MenuContextPriority = 1;
	constexpr float MenuAxisStepThreshold = 0.1f;

	bool IsPausingMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
	{
		return Menu == EGBMenu::Pause || Menu == EGBMenu::Inventory || Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees;
	}
}

AGBPlayerController::AGBPlayerController()
{
	// Hidden while playing; shown while a menu is open (ApplyMouseMode).
	bShowMouseCursor = false;
}

void AGBPlayerController::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (IsLocalController())
	{
		ApplyMouseMode(OpenMenu != EGBMenu::None);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::TickActor(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorTickFunction& ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickActor(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
	if (bCenterCursorPending)
	{
		bCenterCursorPending = false;
		if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::None)
		{
			CenterMouseCursor();
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::ApplyMouseMode(bool bMenuOpen)
{
	if (!IsLocalController())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (bMenuOpen)
	{
		bShowMouseCursor = true;
		FInputModeGameAndUI Mode;
		Mode.SetLockMouseToViewportBehavior(EMouseLockMode::DoNotLock);
		Mode.SetHideCursorDuringCapture(false);
		SetInputMode(Mode);
		return;
	}
	bShowMouseCursor = false;
	// The click that gives the mouse back to the game is swallowed, so it never fires the weapon.
	FInputModeGameOnly Mode;
	Mode.SetConsumeCaptureMouseDown(true);
	SetInputMode(Mode);
}

void AGBPlayerController::CenterMouseCursor()
{
	int32 SizeX = 0;
	int32 SizeY = 0;
	GetViewportSize(SizeX, SizeY);
	if (SizeX > 0 && SizeY > 0)
	{
		SetMouseLocation(SizeX / 2, SizeY / 2);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetupInput(UGBInputSet* InInputSet)
{
	if (!InInputSet)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: SetupInput called without an input set."));
		return;
	}
	const bool bNewSet = InputSet != InInputSet;
	InputSet = InInputSet;

	if (UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer()))
	{
		if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority);
		}
		else
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: no Enhanced Input subsystem, input will not work."));
	}

	UEnhancedInputComponent* EIC = Cast<UEnhancedInputComponent>(InputComponent);
	if (!EIC)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GBPlayerController: the controller input component is not an UEnhancedInputComponent."));
		return;
	}
	if (!bNewSet && EIC->GetActionEventBindings().Num() > 0)
	{
		return; // already bound to this set
	}
	EIC->ClearActionBindings();

	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Inventory, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputInventory);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->SkillTrees, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSkillTrees);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Pause, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputPause);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuNavigate, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuNavigate);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuConfirm, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuConfirm);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuBack, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuBack);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuTab, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuTab);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuReset, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuReset);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuClick, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuClick);
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	if (Menu == OpenMenu)
	{
		return;
	}
	const EGBMenu OldMenu = OpenMenu;
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());

	if (OldMenu != EGBMenu::None && HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuClosed(OldMenu);
	}
	OpenMenu = Menu;

	UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer());
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		SetPause(false);
		if (Subsystem && InputSet)
		{
			Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext);
			// A key or button still held from the menu (the click that closed it) does nothing until released.
			FModifyContextOptions Options;
			Options.bIgnoreAllPressedKeysUntilRelease = true;
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority, Options);
		}
		bCenterCursorPending = false;
		ApplyMouseMode(false);
		return;
	}

	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		if (AGBPlayerCharacter* PlayerCharacter = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetPawn()))
		{
			PlayerCharacter->ReleaseHeldInputs();
		}
	}
	if (Subsystem && InputSet)
	{
		// The death screen also drops the gameplay keys: nothing to do in the world while dead.
		Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext);
		Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
	}
	SetPause(IsPausingMenu(Menu));

	ApplyMouseMode(true);
	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		CenterMouseCursor();
		bCenterCursorPending = true;
	}

	if (HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuOpened(Menu);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::ToggleMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == Menu ? EGBMenu::None : Menu);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputInventory()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::Inventory);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputSkillTrees()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::SkillTrees);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputPause()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None ? EGBMenu::Pause : EGBMenu::None);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuNavigate(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	// Value: x right, y up. One step per press along the dominant axis; the HUD wants y down.
	const FVector2D Axis = Value.Get<FVector2D>();
	if (Axis.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	FIntPoint Step(0, 0);
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis.X) >= FMath::Abs(Axis.Y))
	{
		Step.X = Axis.X > 0.f ? 1 : -1;
	}
	else
	{
		Step.Y = Axis.Y > 0.f ? -1 : 1;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuNavigate(Step);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuConfirm()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
		{
			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
		}
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuConfirm();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuBack()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());
	if (!HUD || !HUD->MenuBack())
	{
		SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuTab(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Axis = Value.Get<float>();
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis) < MenuAxisStepThreshold)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuTab(Axis > 0.f ? 1 : -1);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuReset()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuReset();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuClick()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		// Like confirm: a click anywhere respawns.
		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
		{
			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
		}
		return;
	}
	float MouseX = 0.f;
	float MouseY = 0.f;
	if (!GetMousePosition(MouseX, MouseY))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuClick(FVector2D(MouseX, MouseY));
	}
}

63: 	FString SkillResultText(const UGBDataSubsystem* Data, FName NodeId, EGBSkillUnlockResult Result)
64: 	{
65: 		switch (Result)
66: 		{
67: 		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok: return TEXT("Can be unlocked");
68: 		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::MaxRank: return TEXT("Max rank reached");
69: 		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::NotEnoughPoints: return TEXT("Not enough skill points");
70: 		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode: return TEXT("Unknown skill");
71: 		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::MissingRequirement:
72: 		{
73: 			FString Names;
74: 			if (const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr)
75: 			{
76: 				for (const FName& Req : Def->Requires)
77: 				{
78: 					Names += (Names.IsEmpty() ? TEXT("") : TEXT(", ")) + SkillName(Data, Req);
79: 				}
80: 			}
81: 			return Names.IsEmpty() ? FString(TEXT("Unlock the required skills first")) : FString::Printf(TEXT("Requires: %s"), *Names);
82: 		}
83: 		}
84: 		return FString();
85: 	}

88: 	{
89: 		// FName comparison ignores case.
90: 		return Tree == FName(TEXT("Weapon")) || Tree == FName(TEXT("Weapons"));
91: 	}

104: 		case EGBHeldSlot::Gun: return bPlural ? TEXT("Guns") : TEXT("Gun");
105: 		case EGBHeldSlot::Sword: return TEXT("Sword");

110: 	/** "Damage 10 x8   Fire interval 0.85 s   Magazine 6" from the base data. */
111: 	TArray<FString> WeaponStatLines(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Def)
112: 	{
113: 		TArray<FString> Lines;
114: 		const float Damage = Def.Stats.FindRef(GBStat::Damage);
115: 		const int32 Pellets = FMath::RoundToInt(Def.Stats.FindRef(GBStat::Pellets));
116: 		if (Damage > 0.f)
117: 		{
118: 			Lines.Add(Pellets > 1 ? FString::Printf(TEXT("Damage %d x%d"), FMath::RoundToInt(Damage), Pellets)
119: 				: FString::Printf(TEXT("Damage %d"), FMath::RoundToInt(Damage)));
120: 		}
121: 		const float Interval = Def.Stats.FindRef(GBStat::FireInterval);
122: 		if (Interval > 0.f)
123: 		{
124: 			Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("Fire interval %.2f s"), Interval));
125: 		}
126: 		const int32 Magazine = FMath::RoundToInt(Def.Stats.FindRef(GBStat::MagazineSize));
127: 		if (Def.Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun && Magazine > 0)
128: 		{
129: 			Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("Magazine %d"), Magazine));
130: 		}
131: 		return Lines;
132: 	}

137: AGBPlayerController* AGBHUD::GetGBController() const
138: {
139: 	return Cast<AGBPlayerController>(GetOwningPlayerController());
140: }
141: 
142: EGBMenu AGBHUD::GetOpenMenu() const
143: {
144: 	const AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController();
145: 	return PC ? PC->GetOpenMenu() : EGBMenu::None;
146: }
147: 
148: void AGBHUD::OnMenuOpened(EGBMenu Menu)
149: {
150: 	MenuState = FMenuState();
151: 	MenuOpenTime = GetRealTime();
152: 	ResetMenuMouse();
153: 	if (Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
154: 	{
155: 		// The trees are two pages: open on the one of the held item (gun = weapons, anything else = spells).
156: 		const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
157: 		const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr;
158: 		const AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr;
159: 		SelectSkillTree(Item && Item->GetSlot() == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? 1 : 0);
160: 	}
161: }
162: 
163: int32 AGBHUD::GetShownSkillTree() const
164: {
165: 	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
166: 	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
167: 	return Current ? Current->Tree : 0;
168: }
169: 
170: void AGBHUD::SelectSkillTree(int32 Tree)
171: {
172: 	Tree = FMath::Clamp(Tree, 0, 1);
173: 	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
174: 	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
175: 	if (Current)
176: 	{
177: 		if (Current->Tree == Tree)
178: 		{
179: 			return;
180: 		}
181: 		MenuState.TreeCursor[Current->Tree] = Current->Id;
182: 	}
183: 	const FName Remembered = MenuState.TreeCursor[Tree];
184: 	const FSkillNodeRef* Target = Nodes.FindByPredicate([Remembered, Tree](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Tree == Tree && Node.Id == Remembered; });
185: 	if (!Target)
186: 	{
187: 		Target = Nodes.FindByPredicate([Tree](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Tree == Tree; });
188: 	}
189: 	if (Target)
190: 	{
191: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Target->Id;
192: 	}
193: 	else if (!Current && Nodes.Num() > 0)
194: 	{
195: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes[0].Id;
196: 	}
197: }
198: 
199: void AGBHUD::OnMenuClosed(EGBMenu Menu)
200: {
201: 	MenuState = FMenuState();
202: 	ResetMenuMouse();
203: }
204: 
205: void AGBHUD::SetInventoryPage(int32 NewPage)
206: {
207: 	const FName KeepSkill = MenuState.SkillCursor;
208: 	const FName KeepTree0 = MenuState.TreeCursor[0];
209: 	const FName KeepTree1 = MenuState.TreeCursor[1];
210: 	MenuState = FMenuState();
211: 	MenuState.Page = GBHudMenuLocal::Wrap(NewPage, PageCount);
212: 	MenuState.SkillCursor = KeepSkill;
213: 	MenuState.TreeCursor[0] = KeepTree0;
214: 	MenuState.TreeCursor[1] = KeepTree1;
215: }
216: 
217: // ======================================================================== mouse
218: 
219: void AGBHUD::ResetMenuMouse()
220: {
221: 	MenuHits.Reset();
222: 	MenuHitsMenu = EGBMenu::None;
223: 	MenuHitsPage = -1;
224: 	bMenuHitsChoosing = false;
225: 	// The first position read after opening is only a baseline: an idle mouse must not steal the keyboard cursor.
226: 	bHasMenuMouse = false;
227: }
228: 
229: void AGBHUD::AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind Kind, float X, float Y, float W, float H, int32 Index, FName Id)
230: {
231: 	FMenuHit Hit;
232: 	Hit.Kind = Kind;
233: 	Hit.Min = FVector2D(X, Y);
234: 	Hit.Max = FVector2D(X + FMath::Max(0.f, W), Y + FMath::Max(0.f, H));
235: 	Hit.Index = Index;
236: 	Hit.Id = Id;
237: 	MenuHits.Add(Hit);
238: }
239: 
240: const AGBHUD::FMenuHit* AGBHUD::FindMenuHit(const FVector2D& CanvasPosition) const
241: {
242: 	// Last drawn is on top.
243: 	for (int32 i = MenuHits.Num() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
244: 	{
245: 		const FMenuHit& Hit = MenuHits[i];
246: 		if (CanvasPosition.X >= Hit.Min.X && CanvasPosition.X <= Hit.Max.X && CanvasPosition.Y >= Hit.Min.Y && CanvasPosition.Y <= Hit.Max.Y)
247: 		{
248: 			return &Hit;
249: 		}
250: 	}
251: 	return nullptr;
252: }
253: 
254: void AGBHUD::UpdateMenuMouse()
255: {
256: 	APlayerController* PC = GetOwningPlayerController();
257: 	float MouseX = 0.f;
258: 	float MouseY = 0.f;
259: 	if (!PC || !PC->GetMousePosition(MouseX, MouseY))
260: 	{
261: 		return;
262: 	}
263: 	const FVector2D Mouse(MouseX * MouseToCanvas, MouseY * MouseToCanvas);
264: 	const bool bMoved = bHasMenuMouse && !Mouse.Equals(LastMenuMouse, 0.5f);
265: 	LastMenuMouse = Mouse;
266: 	bHasMenuMouse = true;
267: 	// Hover uses last frame's clickables, so the highlight moves in the same frame it is drawn.
268: 	if (bMoved && MenuHitsMenu == GetOpenMenu())
269: 	{
270: 		if (const FMenuHit* Hit = FindMenuHit(Mouse))
271: 		{
272: 			ApplyMenuHover(*Hit);
273: 		}
274: 	}
275: }
276: 
277: void AGBHUD::ApplyMenuHover(const FMenuHit& Hit)
278: {
279: 	switch (Hit.Kind)
280: 	{
281: 	case EMenuHitKind::PauseEntry:
282: 	case EMenuHitKind::ItemRow:
283: 	case EMenuHitKind::WeaponRow:
284: 		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
285: 		break;
286: 	case EMenuHitKind::SpellSlot:
287: 		if (!MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
288: 		{
289: 			MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
290: 		}
291: 		break;
292: 	case EMenuHitKind::SpellChoice:
293: 		MenuState.SubCursor = Hit.Index;
294: 		break;
295: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillNode:
296: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Hit.Id;
297: 		break;
298: 	case EMenuHitKind::Tab:
299: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillReset:
300: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillAddPoint:
301: 	default:
302: 		// Tabs, the skill buttons (reset, +1 point) and the death screen only react to clicks.
303: 		break;
304: 	}
305: }
306: 
307: void AGBHUD::ActivateMenuHit(const FMenuHit& Hit)
308: {
309: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
310: 	switch (Hit.Kind)
311: 	{
312: 	case EMenuHitKind::PauseEntry:
313: 		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
314: 		MenuConfirm();
315: 		break;
316: 	case EMenuHitKind::Tab:
317: 		if (Hit.Index != MenuState.Page)
318: 		{
319: 			SetInventoryPage(Hit.Index);
320: 		}
321: 		break;
322: 	case EMenuHitKind::ItemRow:
323: 		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
324: 		ConfirmItem(Player);
325: 		break;
326: 	case EMenuHitKind::WeaponRow:
327: 		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
328: 		ConfirmWeapon(Player);
329: 		break;
330: 	case EMenuHitKind::SpellSlot:
331: 		// A click on another slot while choosing leaves the chooser and opens it for that slot.
332: 		MenuState.bChoosingSpell = false;
333: 		MenuState.Cursor = Hit.Index;
334: 		ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
335: 		break;
336: 	case EMenuHitKind::SpellChoice:
337: 		MenuState.SubCursor = Hit.Index;
338: 		ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
339: 		break;
340: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillNode:
341: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Hit.Id;
342: 		ConfirmSkill(Player);
343: 		break;
344: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillReset:
345: 		ResetSkills(Player);
346: 		break;
347: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillAddPoint:
348: 		AddTestSkillPoint(Player);
349: 		break;
350: 	case EMenuHitKind::SkillTreeTab:
351: 		SelectSkillTree(Hit.Index);
352: 		break;
353: 	case EMenuHitKind::DeathScreen:
354: 		// The controller handles the respawn confirm (AGBPlayerController::InputMenuClick): nothing here,
355: 		// so a click respawns exactly once.
356: 		break;
357: 	}
358: }
359: 
360: bool AGBHUD::MenuClick(const FVector2D& ScreenPosition)
361: {
362: 	const EGBMenu Menu = GetOpenMenu();
363: 	// Only the clickables of the screen that is showing right now (not of a page left this frame).
364: 	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None || Menu != MenuHitsMenu || MenuHitsPage != MenuState.Page || bMenuHitsChoosing != MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
365: 	{
366: 		return false;
367: 	}
368: 	const FVector2D Position = ScreenPosition * MouseToCanvas;
369: 	const FMenuHit* Found = FindMenuHit(Position);
370: 	if (!Found)
371: 	{
372: 		return false;
373: 	}
374: 	// Copy: the action may change the menu, and the list is rebuilt on the next draw.
375: 	const FMenuHit Hit = *Found;
376: 	ActivateMenuHit(Hit);
377: 	return true;
378: }
379: 
380: void AGBHUD::MenuNavigate(FIntPoint Direction)
381: {
382: 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
383: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
384: 	switch (GetOpenMenu())
385: 	{
386: 	case EGBMenu::Pause:
387: 		if (Direction.Y != 0)
388: 		{
389: 			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, 2);
390: 		}
391: 		break;
392: 	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
393: 		NavigateSkills(Direction);
394: 		break;
395: 	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
396: 		if (MenuState.Page == PageSkills)
397: 		{
398: 			NavigateSkills(Direction);
399: 		}
400: 		else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
401: 		{
402: 			if (Direction.Y == 0)
403: 			{
404: 				break;
405: 			}
406: 			if (MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
407: 			{
408: 				MenuState.SubCursor = Wrap(MenuState.SubCursor + Direction.Y, GetAssignableSpells(Player).Num());
409: 			}
410: 			else
411: 			{
412: 				MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots + 1);
413: 			}
414: 		}
415: 		else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
416: 		{
417: 			if (Direction.Y != 0)
418: 			{
419: 				MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, GetWeaponList(Player).Num());
420: 			}
421: 		}
422: 		else if (Direction.Y != 0)
423: 		{
424: 			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, GetInventoryList(Player).Num());
425: 		}
426: 		break;
427: 	default:
428: 		break;
429: 	}
430: }
431: 
432: void AGBHUD::MenuConfirm()
433: {
434: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
435: 	switch (GetOpenMenu())
436: 	{
437: 	case EGBMenu::Pause:
438: 		if (MenuState.Cursor == 0)
439: 		{
440: 			if (AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController())
441: 			{
442: 				PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
443: 			}
444: 		}
445: 		else
446: 		{
447: 			UKismetSystemLibrary::QuitGame(this, GetOwningPlayerController(), EQuitPreference::Quit, false);
448: 		}
449: 		break;
450: 	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
451: 		ConfirmSkill(Player);
452: 		break;
453: 	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
454: 		if (MenuState.Page == PageItems)
455: 		{
456: 			ConfirmItem(Player);
457: 		}
458: 		else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
459: 		{
460: 			ConfirmWeapon(Player);
461: 		}
462: 		else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
463: 		{
464: 			ConfirmSpellSlot(Player);
465: 		}
466: 		else
467: 		{
468: 			ConfirmSkill(Player);
469: 		}
470: 		break;
471: 	default:
472: 		// Death: the controller handles the respawn confirm.
473: 		break;
474: 	}
475: }
476: 
477: bool AGBHUD::MenuBack()
478: {
479: 	switch (GetOpenMenu())
480: 	{
481: 	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
482: 		if (MenuState.Page == PageItems && MenuState.PendingCombine != NAME_None)
483: 		{
484: 			MenuState.PendingCombine = NAME_None;
485: 			return true;
486: 		}
487: 		if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells && MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
488: 		{
489: 			MenuState.bChoosingSpell = false;
490: 			return true;
491: 		}
492: 		return false;
493: 	case EGBMenu::Death:
494: 		// The death screen only closes by respawning.
495: 		return true;
496: 	default:
497: 		return false;
498: 	}
499: }
500: 
501: void AGBHUD::MenuTab(int32 Direction)
502: {
503: 	if (Direction == 0)
504: 	{
505: 		return;
506: 	}
507: 	const EGBMenu Menu = GetOpenMenu();
508: 	if (Menu == EGBMenu::Inventory)
509: 	{
510: 		SetInventoryPage(MenuState.Page + Direction);
511: 	}
512: 	else if (Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
513: 	{
514: 		// Two pages: either tab key (Q / E, L1 / L2 / R1) turns to the other tree.
515: 		SelectSkillTree(1 - GetShownSkillTree());
516: 	}
517: }
518: 
519: // ======================================================================== lists
520: 
521: TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetInventoryList(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
522: {
523: 	TArray<FName> Result;
524: 	const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player ? Player->GetInventory() : nullptr;
525: 	if (!Inventory)
526: 	{
527: 		return Result;
528: 	}
529: 	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
530: 	{
531: 		for (const FGBItemDefinition& Def : Data->GetItems())
532: 		{
533: 			if (Inventory->GetCount(Def.Id) > 0)
534: 			{
535: 				Result.Add(Def.Id);
536: 			}
537: 		}
538: 	}
539: 	// Items no longer in the data (after a reload) are still listed, at the end.
540: 	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Entry : Inventory->GetItems())
541: 	{
542: 		if (Entry.Value > 0 && !Result.Contains(Entry.Key))
543: 		{
544: 			Result.Add(Entry.Key);
545: 		}
546: 	}
547: 	return Result;
548: }
549: 
550: TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetWeaponList(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
551: {
552: 	TArray<FName> Result;
553: 	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr;
554: 	if (!Equipment)
555: 	{
556: 		return Result;
557: 	}
558: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
559: 	static const EGBHeldSlot Order[] = { EGBHeldSlot::Staff, EGBHeldSlot::Gun, EGBHeldSlot::Sword };
560: 	for (const EGBHeldSlot Slot : Order)
561: 	{
562: 		for (const FName& WeaponId : Equipment->GetOwnedWeapons())
563: 		{
564: 			const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
565: 			if (Def && Def->Slot == Slot)
566: 			{
567: 				Result.AddUnique(WeaponId);
568: 			}
569: 		}
570: 	}
571: 	// Weapons no longer in the data (after a reload) are still listed, at the end.
572: 	for (const FName& WeaponId : Equipment->GetOwnedWeapons())
573: 	{
574: 		if (WeaponId != NAME_None)
575: 		{
576: 			Result.AddUnique(WeaponId);
577: 		}
578: 	}
579: 	return Result;
580: }
581: 
582: TArray<FName> AGBHUD::GetAssignableSpells(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
583: {
584: 	TArray<FName> Result;
585: 	Result.Add(NAME_None);
586: 	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
587: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
588: 	if (!Spellbook || !Data)
589: 	{
590: 		return Result;
591: 	}
592: 	for (const FName& SpellId : Spellbook->GetKnownSpells())
593: 	{
594: 		const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = Data->FindSpell(SpellId);
595: 		if (Def && !Def->bPassive && !Def->bRequiresGun && SpellId != Spellbook->GetEnchantSpell())
596: 		{
597: 			Result.Add(SpellId);
598: 		}
599: 	}
600: 	return Result;
601: }
602: 
603: TArray<AGBHUD::FSkillNodeRef> AGBHUD::GetSkillNodes() const
604: {
605: 	TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Result;
606: 	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
607: 	{
608: 		for (const FGBSkillNodeDefinition& Def : Data->GetSkillNodes())
609: 		{
610: 			FSkillNodeRef Ref;
611: 			Ref.Id = Def.Id;
612: 			Ref.Tree = GBHudMenuLocal::IsWeaponTree(Def.Tree) ? 1 : 0;
613: 			Ref.Column = FMath::Max(0, Def.Column);
614: 			Ref.Row = FMath::Max(0, Def.Row);
615: 			Result.Add(Ref);
616: 		}
617: 	}
618: 	return Result;
619: }
620: 
621: void AGBHUD::NavigateSkills(FIntPoint Direction)
622: {
623: 	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
624: 	if (Nodes.Num() == 0 || Direction == FIntPoint::ZeroValue)
625: 	{
626: 		return;
627: 	}
628: 	const FSkillNodeRef* Current = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
629: 	if (!Current)
630: 	{
631: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes[0].Id;
632: 		return;
633: 	}
634: 
635: 	// One tree per page: pick the nearest node of the shown tree in the pressed direction.
636: 	auto GridPos = [](const FSkillNodeRef& Node)
637: 	{
638: 		return FVector2D(Node.Column, Node.Row);
639: 	};
640: 	const FVector2D Dir(Direction.X, Direction.Y);
641: 	const FVector2D From = GridPos(*Current);
642: 	float BestCost = TNumericLimits<float>::Max();
643: 	FName Best = NAME_None;
644: 	for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
645: 	{
646: 		if (Node.Id == Current->Id || Node.Tree != Current->Tree)
647: 		{
648: 			continue;
649: 		}
650: 		const FVector2D Delta = GridPos(Node) - From;
651: 		const float Along = FVector2D::DotProduct(Delta, Dir);
652: 		if (Along <= 0.f)
653: 		{
654: 			continue;
655: 		}
656: 		const float Across = FMath::Abs(FVector2D::CrossProduct(Delta, Dir));
657: 		const float Cost = Along + 2.f * Across;
658: 		if (Cost < BestCost)
659: 		{
660: 			BestCost = Cost;
661: 			Best = Node.Id;
662: 		}
663: 	}
664: 	if (Best != NAME_None)
665: 	{
666: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Best;
667: 	}
668: 	else if (Direction.X != 0 && GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Inventory)
669: 	{
670: 		// The inventory's Q / E switch its pages, so there left / right past the tree's edge turns to the other tree
671: 		// (spells on the left, weapons on the right).
672: 		const int32 Other = Direction.X > 0 ? 1 : 0;
673: 		if (Other != Current->Tree)
674: 		{
675: 			SelectSkillTree(Other);
676: 		}
677: 	}
678: }

405: 					Head.Z += 120.f;
406: 				}
407: 				if (FVector::DotProduct(Head - CamLoc, CamFwd) <= 0.f)
408: 				{
409: 					continue;
410: 				}
411: 				const FVector Screen = Canvas->Project(Head, true);
412: 				if (Screen.Z <= 0.f)
413: 				{
414: 					continue;
415: 				}
416: 				TArray<FString> Lines;
417: 				const EGBElement Elements[3] = { EGBElement::Fire, EGBElement::Lightning, EGBElement::Ice };
418: 				const TCHAR* Names[3] = { TEXT("Fire"), TEXT("Lightning"), TEXT("Ice") };
419: 				for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
420: 				{
421: 					const EGBBuildUpPhase Phase = Elemental->GetPhase(Elements[i]);
422: 					if (Phase == EGBBuildUpPhase::Building)
423: 					{
424: 						const float Value = Elemental->GetBuildUp(Elements[i]);
425: 						if (Value <= 0.f)
426: 						{
427: 							continue;
428: 						}
429: 						const float Threshold = Elemental->GetThreshold(Elements[i]);
430: 						Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("%s %d/%d"), Names[i], FMath::RoundToInt(Value), FMath::RoundToInt(Threshold)));
431: 					}
432: 					else if (Phase == EGBBuildUpPhase::Active)
433: 					{
434: 						const FName Reaction = Elemental->GetReaction(Elements[i]);
435: 						Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("%s %s %.1fs"), Names[i], *Reaction.ToString(), Elemental->GetPhaseTimeRemaining(Elements[i])));
436: 					}
437: 					else
438: 					{
439: 						Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("%s IMMUNE %.1fs"), Names[i], Elemental->GetPhaseTimeRemaining(Elements[i])));
440: 					}
441: 				}
442: 				if (Enemy->GetControlLockTime() > 0.f)
443: 				{
444: 					Lines.Add(FString::Printf(TEXT("LOCK %.1fs"), Enemy->GetControlLockTime()));
445: 				}
446: 				float Y = Screen.Y;
447: 				for (const FString& Line : Lines)
448: 				{
449: 					DrawLabel(Line, FLinearColor::White, Screen.X, Y, 14.f, 0.5f, true, false);
450: 					Y += 16.f;
451: 				}
452: 			}
453: 		}
454: 	}
455: 	if (Player)
456: 	{
457: 		DrawHands(Player);
458: 		// Menus cover the game: the status HUD only shows while playing (and under the QTE).
459: 		if (!bMenuOpen && !Player->IsDead())
460: 		{
461: 			if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
462: 			{
463: 				DrawCyberHud(Player);
464: 			}
465: 			else
466: 			{
467: 				DrawRpgHud(Player);
468: 			}
469: 			DrawCrosshair(Player, Skin);
470: 			DrawInteractPrompt(Player, Skin);
471: 		}
472: 	}
473: 	if (!bMenuOpen)
474: 	{
475: 		DrawToasts(Skin);
476: 	}
477: 	if (Player && Player->GetQTE() && Player->GetQTE()->IsActive())
478: 	{
479: 		DrawQTE(Player, Skin);
480: 	}
481: 	DrawDamageFlash(Skin);
482: 	DrawEraFade();
483: 	if (bMenuOpen)
484: 	{
485: 		UpdateMenuMouse();
486: 		DrawMenus(Player);
487: 		// Menu actions answer with toasts (skill unlocks, combines): above the menu, low, clear of titles and tabs.
488: 		DrawToasts(GetMenuSkin(), Canvas->ClipY * 0.80f);
489: 	}
490: }

494: void AGBHUD::DrawHands(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
495: {
// Which era the world is in, and the travel between eras (fade out, swap, fade in).
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Subsystems/WorldSubsystem.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "GBEraSubsystem.generated.h"

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnEraChanged, EGBEra /*NewEra*/);

/** Keys of Game.json "Values" read by the era travel (seconds, real time). */
namespace GBGameValue
{
	inline const FName EraFadeOutTime(TEXT("EraFadeOutTime"));
	inline const FName EraHoldTime(TEXT("EraHoldTime"));
	inline const FName EraFadeInTime(TEXT("EraFadeInTime"));
	/** Era travel restore: health and mana are raised to at least this fraction of max (never lowered), and whether guns are refilled. */
	inline const FName EraTravelHealthFraction(TEXT("EraTravelHealthFraction"));
	inline const FName EraTravelManaFraction(TEXT("EraTravelManaFraction"));
	inline const FName EraTravelRefillAmmo(TEXT("EraTravelRefillAmmo"));
}

/**
 * One map, two times (GDD 4). The swap is done two ways at once, so level design can use either:
 *  - actors with a UGBEraComponent appear / disappear with their era;
 *  - streaming sub-levels whose package name ends in "_Past" or "_Future" are shown / hidden with their era.
 * Travel is a fade (Game.json: EraFadeOutTime, EraHoldTime, EraFadeInTime) that hides the swap: the era changes
 * while the screen is fully covered. On arrival everything of the player is restored (health, mana, ammo) and a
 * checkpoint is saved: portals are the safe points of the map.
 */
UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBEraSubsystem : public UTickableWorldSubsystem
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	static UGBEraSubsystem* Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject);

	EGBEra GetCurrentEra() const { return CurrentEra; }

	/** Changes era at once, no fade (level start, checkpoint restore). Broadcasts OnEraChanged. */
	void SetEraImmediate(EGBEra NewEra);

	/**
	 * Starts the travel fade to TargetEra. If bTeleport, the traveller is moved to Destination while the screen
	 * is covered. Returns false if a travel is already running.
	 */
	bool RequestTravel(EGBEra TargetEra, AActor* Traveller, bool bTeleport = false, const FTransform& Destination = FTransform::Identity);

	bool IsTravelling() const { return bTravelling; }

	/** 0 = clear, 1 = screen fully covered. The HUD draws it. */
	float GetFadeAlpha() const { return FadeAlpha; }

	/** True if something with this presence exists in Era. */
	static bool IsPresentIn(EGBEraPresence Presence, EGBEra Era);
	static EGBEra OtherEra(EGBEra Era) { return Era == EGBEra::Past ? EGBEra::Future : EGBEra::Past; }

	/** Fired after the swap (actors and sub-levels already updated), with the screen still covered when travelling. */
	FGBOnEraChanged OnEraChanged;
	/** Fired when a travel has fully finished (fade in done). */
	FGBOnEraChanged OnTravelFinished;

	// UTickableWorldSubsystem
	virtual void Tick(float DeltaTime) override;
	virtual TStatId GetStatId() const override;
	virtual bool DoesSupportWorldType(const EWorldType::Type WorldType) const override;
	/** Applies the starting era to the "_Past" / "_Future" sub-levels. */
	virtual void OnWorldBeginPlay(UWorld& InWorld) override;

protected:
	void ApplyEra();
	/** The moment the screen is fully covered: swap era, teleport, restore and save. A dead traveller undoes the travel instead (stays in the current era, no teleport / restore / save). */
	void PerformSwap();
	void FinishTravel();
	/** Fade durations from Game.json, read when the travel starts. */
	float FadeOutTime = 0.f;
	float HoldTime = 0.f;
	float FadeInTime = 0.f;

	EGBEra CurrentEra = EGBEra::Past;
	bool bTravelling = false;
	float FadeAlpha = 0.f;
	/** 0 fading out, 1 holding, 2 fading in. */
	int32 TravelStage = 0;
	float StageTime = 0.f;
	EGBEra PendingEra = EGBEra::Past;
	TWeakObjectPtr<AActor> PendingTraveller;
	bool bPendingTeleport = false;
	FTransform PendingDestination;
};
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Engine/LevelStreaming.h"
#include "GameFramework/Controller.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/MovementComponent.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

namespace GBEraSubsystemLocal
{
	// A hitch (loading, breakpoint) must not skip the whole fade in one frame.
	constexpr float MaxFadeStep = 0.1f;
}

UGBEraSubsystem* UGBEraSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject || !GEngine)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull);
	return World ? World->GetSubsystem<UGBEraSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::DoesSupportWorldType(const EWorldType::Type WorldType) const
{
	return WorldType == EWorldType::Game || WorldType == EWorldType::PIE;
}

TStatId UGBEraSubsystem::GetStatId() const
{
	RETURN_QUICK_DECLARE_CYCLE_STAT(UGBEraSubsystem, STATGROUP_Tickables);
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::OnWorldBeginPlay(UWorld& InWorld)
{
	Super::OnWorldBeginPlay(InWorld);
	ApplyEra();
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(EGBEraPresence Presence, EGBEra Era)
{
	switch (Presence)
	{
	case EGBEraPresence::PastOnly:   return Era == EGBEra::Past;
	case EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly: return Era == EGBEra::Future;
	default:                         return true;
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::SetEraImmediate(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	CurrentEra = NewEra;
	ApplyEra();
}

1486: 			TotalW += Widths.Last() + 3.f * P;
1487: 		}
1488: 		float TabX = FMath::RoundToFloat((Screen.X + Screen.W * 0.5f - TotalW * 0.5f) / P) * P;
1489: 		const float TabY = Plaque.Max.Y + 3.f * P;
1490: 		const float TabH = 7.f * TabScale + 8.f * P;
1491: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < PageCount; ++i)
1492: 		{
1493: 			const bool bSelected = MenuState.Page == i;
1494: 			const bool bHover = IsMouseOver(TabX, TabY, Widths[i], TabH);
1495: 			FillRect(TabX, TabY, Widths[i], TabH, GBRpg::Stone1);
1496: 			RpgStone(TabX, TabY, Widths[i], TabH, bSelected ? FLinearColor(1.3f, 1.15f, 1.f, 1.f) : FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.85f, 1.f));
1497: 			RpgFrame(TabX, TabY, Widths[i], TabH, bSelected ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Iron);
1498: 			if (bSelected)
1499: 			{
1500: 				DrawGlow(TabX + Widths[i] * 0.5f, TabY + TabH, Widths[i] * 0.7f, TabH, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.18f));
1501: 			}
1502: 			FPixelTextStyle Style;
1503: 			Style.Scale = TabScale;
1504: 			Style.Ink = bSelected ? &GBRpg::InkGold : (bHover ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr);
1505: 			Style.Color = bSelected || bHover ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDim;
1506: 			Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
1507: 			DrawPixelText(FString(Tabs[i]).ToUpper(), TabX + Widths[i] * 0.5f, TabY + 4.f * P, Style, 0.5f);
1508: 			AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::Tab, TabX, TabY, Widths[i], TabH, i);
1509: 			TabX += Widths[i] + 3.f * P;
1510: 		}
1511: 		float KW = 0.f;
1512: 		RpgKeyCap(FMath::RoundToFloat((Screen.X + Screen.W * 0.5f - TotalW * 0.5f) / P) * P - 13.f * P, TabY + 3.f * P, TEXT("Q"), &KW);
1513: 		RpgKeyCap(TabX + 2.f * P, TabY + 3.f * P, TEXT("E"));
1514: 		CX = Screen.X + 16.f * P;
1515: 		CY = TabY + TabH + 8.f * P;
1516: 		CW = Screen.W - 32.f * P;
1517: 	}
1518: 
1519: 	TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Hints;
1520: 	Hints.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Q E"), TEXT("Page")));
1521: 	Hints.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Select")));
1522: 	Hints.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Esc"), MenuState.bChoosingSpell || MenuState.PendingCombine != NAME_None ? TEXT("Back") : TEXT("Close")));
1523: 	if (MenuState.Page == PageSkills)
1524: 	{
1525: 		Hints.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("R"), TEXT("Reset skills")));
1526: 	}
1527: 	MenuHints(Skin, Hints, bCyber ? Screen.X : W * 0.5f, Screen.FooterY, bCyber ? 0.f : 0.5f);
1528: 
1529: 	if (!Player)
1530: 	{
1531: 		return;
1532: 	}
1533: 	const float CH = Screen.FooterY - (bCyber ? 26.f * S : 5.f * P) - CY;
1534: 	if (MenuState.Page == PageItems)
1535: 	{
1536: 		DrawItemsPage(Player, Skin, CX, CY, CW, CH);
1537: 	}
1538: 	else if (MenuState.Page == PageWeapons)
1539: 	{
1540: 		DrawWeaponsPage(Player, Skin, CX, CY, CW, CH);
1541: 	}
1542: 	else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
1543: 	{
1544: 		DrawSpellsPage(Player, Skin, CX, CY, CW, CH);
1545: 	}
1546: 	else
1547: 	{
1548: 		DrawSkillTrees(Player, Skin, CX, CY, CW, CH);
1549: 	}
1550: }
1551: 
1552: // ======================================================================== drawing: pages
1553: 
1554: void AGBHUD::DrawItemsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
1555: {
1556: 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
1557: 	const float S = UIScale();
1558: 	const float P = ArtPixel();
1559: 	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
1560: 	const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory();
2205: 
2206: void AGBHUD::DrawSkillTrees(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
2207: {
2208: 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
2209: 	const float S = UIScale();
2210: 	const float P = ArtPixel();
2211: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
2212: 	const UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree();
2213: 	if (!SkillTree)
2214: 	{
2215: 		return;
2216: 	}
2217: 	const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
2218: 	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
2219: 
2220: 	// Top row: points in the middle, "+1 skill point" (test) on the left, reset on the right.
2221: 	{
2222: 		const int32 Points = SkillTree->GetPoints();
2223: 		const FString PointsText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), Points);
2224: 		const float RowY = Y;
2225: 		const float BtnH = bCyber ? 36.f * S : 13.f * P;
2226: 		const float CX = X + W * 0.5f;
2227: 		const FVector2D LabelSize = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, TEXT("SKILL POINTS"));
2228: 		const FVector2D NumSize = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Heading, PointsText);
2229: 		const float GroupW = LabelSize.X + (bCyber ? 14.f * S : 4.f * P) + NumSize.X;
2230: 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Muted, TEXT("SKILL POINTS"), CX - GroupW * 0.5f, RowY + (BtnH - LabelSize.Y) * 0.5f);
2231: 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Heading, Points > 0 ? EGBTextTone::Warn : EGBTextTone::Accent, PointsText, CX + GroupW * 0.5f - NumSize.X, RowY + (BtnH - NumSize.Y) * 0.5f);
2232: 		const int32 Spent = SkillTree->GetPointsSpent();
2233: 		const FString ResetText = FString::Printf(TEXT("Reset skills (+%d)"), Spent);
2234: 		const FString AddText(TEXT("+1 skill point"));
2235: 		const float ResetW = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, ResetText).X + (bCyber ? 40.f * S : 14.f * P);
2236: 		const float AddW = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, AddText).X + (bCyber ? 40.f * S : 14.f * P);
2237: 		const float Margin = bCyber ? 40.f * S : 12.f * P;
2238: 		const float ResetX = CX + GroupW * 0.5f + Margin;
2239: 		const float AddX = CX - GroupW * 0.5f - Margin - AddW;
2240: 		MenuButton(Skin, ResetX, RowY, ResetW, BtnH, ResetText, Spent > 0, IsMouseOver(ResetX, RowY, ResetW, BtnH));
2241: 		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SkillReset, ResetX, RowY, ResetW, BtnH);
2242: 		MenuButton(Skin, AddX, RowY, AddW, BtnH, AddText, true, IsMouseOver(AddX, RowY, AddW, BtnH));
2243: 		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SkillAddPoint, AddX, RowY, AddW, BtnH);
2244: 		Y += BtnH + (bCyber ? 18.f * S : 6.f * P);
2245: 		H -= BtnH + (bCyber ? 18.f * S : 6.f * P);
2246: 	}
2247: 	if (Nodes.Num() == 0)
2248: 	{
2249: 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Muted, TEXT("No skills defined (Skills.json)."), X + W * 0.5f, Y + 20.f * S, 0.5f);
2250: 		return;
2251: 	}
2252: 	const FSkillNodeRef* Selected = Nodes.FindByPredicate([this](const FSkillNodeRef& Node) { return Node.Id == MenuState.SkillCursor; });
2253: 	if (!Selected)
2254: 	{
2255: 		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes[0].Id;
2256: 		Selected = &Nodes[0];
2257: 	}
2258: 
2259: 	// GDD 13: the spell tree has the 90s RPG look, the weapon tree the cyberpunk look, whatever the held item.
2260: 	// One tree per page (owner, Nov 13): two tabs, each in its own tree's look, then the shown tree at full width.
2261: 	static const TCHAR* Titles[] = { TEXT("SPELLS"), TEXT("WEAPONS") };
2262: 	const int32 ShownTree = Selected->Tree == 1 ? 1 : 0;
2263: 	{
2264: 		const int32 TabScale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(3.f * S));
2265: 		FCyTextStyle CyTab;
2266: 		CyTab.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
2267: 		CyTab.Px = 22.f;
2268: 		CyTab.Spacing = 140;
2269: 		CyTab.bUpper = true;
2270: 		const float TabH = FMath::RoundToFloat(FMath::Max(40.f * S, 7.f * TabScale + 8.f * P) / P) * P;
2271: 		const float SpellW = FMath::CeilToFloat((MeasurePixelText(Titles[0], TabScale).X + 20.f * P) / P) * P;
2272: 		const float WeaponW = FMath::CeilToFloat(MeasureCyText(Titles[1], CyTab).X + 48.f * S);
2273: 		const float TabGap = FMath::RoundToFloat(10.f * S);
2274: 		const float TabX0 = FMath::RoundToFloat((X + W * 0.5f - (SpellW + TabGap + WeaponW) * 0.5f) / P) * P;
2275: 		const float TabY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y / P) * P;
2276: 		// Spells: a stone tab with a gold frame when shown.
2277: 		{
2278: 			const bool bShown = ShownTree == 0;
2279: 			const bool bHover = IsMouseOver(TabX0, TabY, SpellW, TabH);
2280: 			FillRect(TabX0, TabY, SpellW, TabH, GBRpg::Stone1);
2281: 			RpgStone(TabX0, TabY, SpellW, TabH, bShown ? FLinearColor(1.3f, 1.15f, 1.f, 1.f) : FLinearColor(0.7f, 0.7f, 0.75f, 1.f));
2282: 			RpgFrame(TabX0, TabY, SpellW, TabH, bShown ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Iron);
2283: 			if (bShown)
2284: 			{
2285: 				DrawGlow(TabX0 + SpellW * 0.5f, TabY + TabH, SpellW * 0.7f, TabH, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.18f));
2286: 			}
2287: 			FPixelTextStyle Style;
2288: 			Style.Scale = TabScale;
2289: 			Style.Ink = bShown ? &GBRpg::InkGold : (bHover ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr);
2290: 			Style.Color = bShown || bHover ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDim;
2291: 			Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
2292: 			DrawPixelText(Titles[0], TabX0 + SpellW * 0.5f, TabY + FMath::RoundToFloat((TabH - 7.f * TabScale) * 0.5f / P) * P, Style, 0.5f);
2293: 			AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SkillTreeTab, TabX0, TabY, SpellW, TabH, 0);
2294: 		}
2295: 		// Weapons: a cut-corner cyberpunk tab, filled red when shown.
2296: 		{
2297: 			const float TX = TabX0 + SpellW + TabGap;
2298: 			const bool bShown = ShownTree == 1;
2299: 			const bool bHover = IsMouseOver(TX, TabY, WeaponW, TabH);
2300: 			if (bShown)
2301: 			{
2302: 				CyBox(TX, TabY, WeaponW, TabH, 10.f * S, GBCy::Red, FLinearColor::Transparent, 0x4);
2303: 			}
2304: 			else
2305: 			{
2306: 				CyBox(TX, TabY, WeaponW, TabH, 10.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.85f), bHover ? GBCy::RedBright : GBCy::RedDeep, 0x4);
2307: 			}
2308: 			CyTab.Color = bShown ? GBCy::Ink : (bHover ? GBCy::Pale : GBCy::RedDim);
2309: 			const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Titles[1], CyTab);
2310: 			DrawCyText(Titles[1], TX + WeaponW * 0.5f, TabY + (TabH - Size.Y) * 0.5f, CyTab, 0.5f);
2311: 			AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SkillTreeTab, TX, TabY, WeaponW, TabH, 1);
2312: 		}
2313: 		// The tab keys on both sides, in the screen's look (the inventory's skills page uses its own Q / E for pages).
2314: 		if (GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
2315: 		{
2316: 			if (bCyber)
2317: 			{
2318: 				CyKey(TabX0 - 36.f * S, TabY + (TabH - 22.f * S) * 0.5f, TEXT("Q"), GBCy::Cyan, 22.f);
2319: 				CyKey(TabX0 + SpellW + TabGap + WeaponW + 12.f * S, TabY + (TabH - 22.f * S) * 0.5f, TEXT("E"), GBCy::Cyan, 22.f);
2320: 			}
2321: 			else
2322: 			{
2323: 				RpgKeyCap(TabX0 - 13.f * P, TabY + 3.f * P, TEXT("Q"));
2324: 				RpgKeyCap(TabX0 + SpellW + TabGap + WeaponW + 3.f * P, TabY + 3.f * P, TEXT("E"));
2325: 			}
2326: 		}
2327: 		const float Below = TabH + (bCyber ? 14.f * S : 5.f * P);
2328: 		Y += Below;
2329: 		H -= Below;
2330: 	}
2331: 
2332: 	const float DetailH = FMath::RoundToFloat(FMath::Max(110.f * S, 26.f * P));
2333: 	const float AreaW = W;
2334: 	const float AreaY = Y;
2335: 	const float AreaH = FMath::Max(100.f * S, H - DetailH - 12.f * S);
2336: 
2337: 	for (int32 Tree = ShownTree; Tree == ShownTree; ++Tree)
2338: 	{
2339: 		const float AX = X;
2340: 		const bool bTreeCyber = Tree == 1;
2341: 		const EGBHudSkin TreeSkin = bTreeCyber ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
2342: 		float GridTop = AreaY;
2343: 		if (bTreeCyber)
2344: 		{
2345: 			CyBox(AX, AreaY, AreaW, AreaH, 22.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.92f), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.9f), 0x5);
2346: 			CyScanlines(AX, AreaY, AreaW, AreaH, 0.25f);
2347: 			DrawGlow(AX + AreaW * 0.5f, AreaY, AreaW * 0.6f, AreaH * 0.4f, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.08f));
2348: 			FCyTextStyle TitleStyle;
2349: 			TitleStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
2350: 			TitleStyle.Px = 26.f;
2351: 			TitleStyle.Spacing = 200;
2352: 			TitleStyle.bUpper = true;
2353: 			TitleStyle.Color = GBCy::Red;
2354: 			const FVector2D TS = DrawCyText(Titles[Tree], AX + 26.f * S, AreaY + 16.f * S, TitleStyle, 0.f);
2355: 			FCyTextStyle Sub;
2356: 			Sub.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
2357: 			Sub.Px = 12.f;
2358: 			Sub.Color = GBCy::RedDim;
2359: 			DrawCyText(TEXT("// FIREARMS PERKS"), AX + 26.f * S + TS.X + 14.f * S, AreaY + 26.f * S, Sub, 0.f);
2360: 			CyRuler(AX + 26.f * S, AreaY + 16.f * S + TS.Y + 8.f * S, AreaW - 52.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.7f));
2361: 			GridTop = AreaY + 16.f * S + TS.Y + 30.f * S;
2362: 		}
2363: 		else
2364: 		{
2365: 			const float PX = FMath::RoundToFloat(AX / P) * P;
2366: 			const float PY = FMath::RoundToFloat(AreaY / P) * P;
2367: 			const float PW = FMath::RoundToFloat(AreaW / P) * P;
2368: 			const float PH = FMath::RoundToFloat(AreaH / P) * P;
2369: 			FillRect(PX, PY, PW, PH, GBRpg::Stone1);
2370: 			RpgStone(PX, PY, PW, PH);
2371: 			DrawGlow(PX + PW * 0.5f, PY + PH * 0.35f, PW * 0.6f, PH * 0.6f, FLinearColor(0.35f, 0.2f, 0.1f, 0.12f));
2372: 			RpgFrame(PX, PY, PW, PH, ERpgFrame::Iron);
2373: 			DrawOutline(PX + 4.f * P, PY + 4.f * P, PW - 8.f * P, PH - 8.f * P, GBRpg::Gold1, P);
2374: 			const int32 HeadScale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(4.f * S));
2375: 			FPixelTextStyle Head;
2376: 			Head.Scale = HeadScale;
2377: 			Head.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
2378: 			Head.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
2379: 			Head.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.7f);
2380: 			DrawPixelText(Titles[Tree], PX + PW * 0.5f, PY + 8.f * P, Head, 0.5f);
2381: 			GridTop = PY + 8.f * P + 7.f * HeadScale + 6.f * P;
2382: 		}
2383: 
2384: 		int32 MaxColumn = 0;
2385: 		int32 MaxRow = 0;
2386: 		for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
2387: 		{
2388: 			if (Node.Tree == Tree)
2389: 			{
2390: 				MaxColumn = FMath::Max(MaxColumn, Node.Column);
2391: 				MaxRow = FMath::Max(MaxRow, Node.Row);
2392: 			}
2393: 		}
2394: 		const float Pad = bTreeCyber ? 22.f * S : 9.f * P;
2395: 		// The spell tree names its four element columns above the nodes.
2396: 		const float ColumnHeadH = bTreeCyber ? 0.f : 8.f * P;
2397: 		const float GridY = GridTop + ColumnHeadH;
2398: 		// The page is the full width: cap the cell width so the boxes keep their size, and centre the grid.
2399: 		const float MaxCellW = bTreeCyber ? 330.f * S : 52.f * P;
2400: 		const float CellW = FMath::Min(MaxCellW, (AreaW - 2.f * Pad) / (MaxColumn + 1));
2401: 		const float GridX = FMath::RoundToFloat(AX + (AreaW - CellW * (MaxColumn + 1)) * 0.5f);
2402: 		const float CellH = FMath::Min(bTreeCyber ? 104.f * S : 29.f * P, (AreaY + AreaH - Pad - GridY) / (MaxRow + 1));
2403: 		const float BoxW = FMath::Max(20.f * S, CellW - (bTreeCyber ? 18.f * S : 5.f * P));
2404: 		const float BoxH = FMath::Max(20.f * S, CellH - (bTreeCyber ? 22.f * S : 4.f * P));
2405: 		auto BoxPos = [&](const FSkillNodeRef& Node)
2406: 		{
2407: 			return FVector2D(GridX + Node.Column * CellW + (CellW - BoxW) * 0.5f, GridY + Node.Row * CellH);
2408: 		};
2409: 		if (!bTreeCyber)
2410: 		{
2411: 			for (int32 Column = 0; Column <= MaxColumn; ++Column)
2412: 			{
2413: 				const float ColX = GridX + Column * CellW + CellW * 0.5f;
2414: 				const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(GBHudMenuDraw::SpellColumnElement(Column));
2415: 				const FString Label = FString(GBHudMenuDraw::SpellColumnName(Column)).ToUpper();
2416: 				const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
2417: 				const float LW = MeasurePixelText(Label, Small).X;
2418: 				RpgGem(ColX - LW * 0.5f - 5.f * P, GridTop + 2.5f * P, EC, true);
2419: 				FPixelTextStyle Lab;
2420: 				Lab.Scale = Small;
2421: 				Lab.Color = EC;
2422: 				Lab.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
2423: 				DrawPixelText(Label, ColX + 2.f * P, GridTop, Lab, 0.5f);
2424: 			}
2425: 		}
2426: 
2427: 		// Requirement links first, under the boxes: down from the parent, across in the gap above the child, down.
2428: 		for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
2429: 		{
2430: 			const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = (Node.Tree == Tree && Data) ? Data->FindSkillNode(Node.Id) : nullptr;
2431: 			if (!Def)
2432: 			{
2433: 				continue;
2434: 			}
2435: 			for (const FName& Req : Def->Requires)
2436: 			{
2437: 				const FSkillNodeRef* ReqNode = Nodes.FindByPredicate([&Req](const FSkillNodeRef& Other) { return Other.Id == Req; });
2438: 				if (!ReqNode || ReqNode->Tree != Tree)
2439: 				{
2440: 					continue;
2441: 				}
2442: 				const FVector2D A = BoxPos(*ReqNode) + FVector2D(BoxW * 0.5f, BoxH);
2443: 				const FVector2D B = BoxPos(Node) + FVector2D(BoxW * 0.5f, 0.f);
2444: 				const bool bOwned = SkillTree->GetRank(Req) > 0;
2445: 				const float ElbowY = FMath::RoundToFloat(B.Y - (CellH - BoxH) * 0.5f);
2446: 				if (bTreeCyber)
2447: 				{
2448: 					const FLinearColor LinkColor = bOwned ? GBCy::Red : GBAlpha(GBCy::Grey, 0.45f);
2449: 					const float T = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat((bOwned ? 2.f : 1.f) * S));
2450: 					FillRect(A.X - T * 0.5f, A.Y, T, ElbowY - A.Y, LinkColor);
2451: 					FillRect(FMath::Min(A.X, B.X) - T * 0.5f, ElbowY - T * 0.5f, FMath::Abs(B.X - A.X) + T, T, LinkColor);
2452: 					FillRect(B.X - T * 0.5f, ElbowY, T, B.Y - ElbowY, LinkColor);
2453: 					FillRect(B.X - 3.f * S, B.Y - 6.f * S, 6.f * S, 6.f * S, LinkColor);
2454: 					if (bOwned)
2455: 					{
2456: 						// A data pulse running down the owned link.
2457: 						const float Len = (ElbowY - A.Y) + FMath::Abs(B.X - A.X) + (B.Y - ElbowY);
2458: 						const float D = FMath::Frac((float)GetRealTime() * 0.6f + A.X * 0.001f) * Len;
2459: 						FVector2D Dot;
2460: 						if (D < ElbowY - A.Y) { Dot = FVector2D(A.X, A.Y + D); }
2461: 						else if (D < ElbowY - A.Y + FMath::Abs(B.X - A.X)) { Dot = FVector2D(A.X + FMath::Sign(B.X - A.X) * (D - (ElbowY - A.Y)), ElbowY); }
2462: 						else { Dot = FVector2D(B.X, ElbowY + (D - (ElbowY - A.Y) - FMath::Abs(B.X - A.X))); }
2463: 						DrawGlow(Dot.X, Dot.Y, 8.f * S, 8.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedBright, 0.9f));
2464: 					}
2465: 				}
2466: 				else
2467: 				{
2468: 					// A gilt chain two art pixels wide with a dark keyline.
2469: 					const float T = 2.f * P;
2470: 					const float AX2 = FMath::RoundToFloat((A.X - P) / P) * P;
2471: 					const float BX2 = FMath::RoundToFloat((B.X - P) / P) * P;
2472: 					const float EY = FMath::RoundToFloat(ElbowY / P) * P;
2473: 					const FLinearColor Core = bOwned ? GBRpg::Gold3 : GBRpg::Iron1;
2474: 					auto Seg = [&](float SX, float SY, float SW, float SH)
2475: 					{
2476: 						FillRect(SX - P, SY - P, SW + 2.f * P, SH + 2.f * P, GBRpg::Outline);
2477: 					};
2478: 					auto SegCore = [&](float SX, float SY, float SW, float SH) { FillRect(SX, SY, SW, SH, Core); };
2479: 					Seg(AX2, A.Y, T, EY - A.Y);
2480: 					Seg(FMath::Min(AX2, BX2), EY, FMath::Abs(BX2 - AX2) + T, T);
2481: 					Seg(BX2, EY, T, B.Y - EY);
2482: 					SegCore(AX2, A.Y, T, EY - A.Y);
2483: 					SegCore(FMath::Min(AX2, BX2), EY, FMath::Abs(BX2 - AX2) + T, T);
2484: 					SegCore(BX2, EY, T, B.Y - EY);
2485: 				}
2486: 			}
2487: 		}
2488: 		for (const FSkillNodeRef& Node : Nodes)
2489: 		{
2490: 			if (Node.Tree != Tree)
2491: 			{
2492: 				continue;
2493: 			}
2494: 			const FVector2D Pos = BoxPos(Node);
2495: 			DrawSkillNode(Player, Tree, Node.Id, Pos.X, Pos.Y, BoxW, BoxH);
2496: 			AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SkillNode, Pos.X, Pos.Y, BoxW, BoxH, 0, Node.Id);
2497: 		}
2498: 		(void)TreeSkin;
2499: 	}
2500:
